@layer base, reactive;

/* Registered, so it transitions: the count as it is drawn, easing toward the real one. */
@property --shown {
  syntax: "<number>";
  inherits: true;
  initial-value: 97;
}

/* Timers for hold-to-repeat: they change nothing visible, only their animationend counts. */
@keyframes rc-wait {
  to {
    --rc-t: 1;
  }
}
@keyframes rc-a {
  to {
    --rc-t: 1;
  }
}
@keyframes rc-b {
  to {
    --rc-t: 1;
  }
}

@layer base {
  :root {
    color-scheme: dark;
  }
  * {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    margin: 0;
  }

  body {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    min-block-size: 100dvh;
    padding: 24px 16px;
    background: oklch(19.5% 0 0);
    font-family:
      system-ui,
      -apple-system,
      "Segoe UI",
      sans-serif;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  }

  .counter {
    --b: clamp(64px, 17vw, 92px); /* button size */
    --shown: var(--n, 97);
    /* The distance still to go, 0 to 1: the wheels blur by it, so they are sharp at rest and soft when spinning. */
    --blur: min(
      1,
      max(var(--n, 97) - var(--shown), var(--shown) - var(--n, 97))
    );
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: clamp(8px, 2vw, 14px);
    padding: 8px;
    border-radius: calc(var(--b) * 0.32);
    background: oklch(23.5% 0 0);
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 1px 0 oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.04),
      0 10px 30px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.35);
    transition: --shown 650ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
  }

  .step {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    inline-size: var(--b);
    aspect-ratio: 1;
    padding: 0;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: calc(var(--b) * 0.24);
    background: oklch(30% 0 0);
    color: oklch(96% 0 0);
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-user-select: none;
    -webkit-touch-callout: none; /* a long press on a phone holds to repeat, without the system menu */
    touch-action: manipulation;
    transition:
      background-color 150ms,
      scale 150ms,
      opacity 200ms;
    svg {
      inline-size: 34%;
      fill: none;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 1.8px;
      stroke-linecap: round;
    }
    &:hover {
      background: oklch(34% 0 0);
    }
    &:active {
      scale: 0.94;
    }
    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid oklch(80% 0 0);
      outline-offset: 3px;
    }
  }

  /* The digits: three windows side by side; a hidden one is 0 wide. */
  .value {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    min-inline-size: calc(var(--b) * 1.2);
    color: oklch(97% 0 0);
    font:
      500 calc(var(--b) * 0.72)/1 system-ui,
      -apple-system,
      "Segoe UI",
      sans-serif;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  }
  .reel {
    --cell: 1.2em;
    inline-size: calc(0.62em * var(--vis, 1));
    block-size: var(--cell);
    overflow: hidden;
    opacity: var(--vis, 1);
    mask: linear-gradient(
      transparent,
      black 22%,
      black 78%,
      transparent
    ); /* digits fade in and out at the edges */
  }
  .strip {
    display: grid;
    justify-items: center;
    translate: 0 calc(var(--d)  *-1*  var(--cell));
    filter: blur(calc(var(--blur) * 1.4px));
    i {
      block-size: var(--cell);
      line-height: var(--cell);
      font-style: normal;
    }
  }
  /* Each wheel's position, from --shown: units turn all the time; tens and hundreds only on the carry (the last step
     of the wheel below, from 9 to 0). Leading wheels fold away below 10 and 100. */
  .u {
    --d: mod(var(--shown), 10);
  }
  .t {
    --d: mod(
      round(down, var(--shown) / 10) + clamp(0, mod(var(--shown), 10) - 9, 1),
      10
    );
    --vis: clamp(0, var(--shown) - 9, 1);
  }
  .h {
    --d: mod(
      round(down, var(--shown) / 100) + clamp(0, mod(var(--shown), 100) - 99, 1),
      10
    );
    --vis: clamp(0, var(--shown) - 99, 1);
  }

  /* For screen readers: the count as text, announced when it changes. */
  .sr {
    position: absolute;
    inline-size: 1px;
    block-size: 1px;
    overflow: hidden;
    clip-path: inset(50%);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .sr::after {
    counter-reset: n var(--n, 97);
    content: counter(n);
  }

  body {
    display: block;
    padding: 0 16px;
  }
  .counter {
    align-self: center;
  }
  .colophon,
  .labels {
    --card-heading: oklch(88% 0 0);
    --card-lead: oklch(86% 0 0);
    --card-text: oklch(64% 0 0);
    --card-faint: oklch(50% 0 0);
    --card-code: oklch(90% 0 0);
    --card-link: white;
    --card-bg: oklch(18% 0 0 / 0.72);
    --card-border: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.1);
    --card-line: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.07);
    --card-chip: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.07);
    --card-chip-hover: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.16);
    --card-shadow: oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.8);
    --card-focus: oklch(80% 0 0);
    --pill-bg: oklch(22% 0 0);
    --pill-ink: oklch(72% 0 0);
    --pill-strong: white;
    --pill-border: oklch(32% 0 0);
    --pill-hover: oklch(30% 0 0);
    --site-bg: oklch(94% 0 0);
    --site-ink: oklch(20% 0 0);
    --site-hover: white;
    --pill-shadow: oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.5);
  }
  /* The explanation: a card set apart from the piece. A lead sentence, then numbered points; the functions are chips
     that link to their reference pages, so it reads as notes about the page, not part of it. */
  .colophon {
    inline-size: min(100%, 680px);
    font:
      14px/1.6 system-ui,
      sans-serif;
    color: var(--card-text);
  }
  .colophon-head {
    display: grid;
    justify-items: center;
    gap: 4px;
    h2 {
      font:
        650 15px/1.3 system-ui,
        sans-serif;
      letter-spacing: -0.01em;
      color: var(--card-heading);
    }
    .down {
      display: grid;
      place-items: center;
      inline-size: 40px;
      block-size: 40px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      color: var(--card-faint);
      animation: nudge 2.2s ease-in-out infinite;
      &:hover {
        color: var(--card-link);
      }
      &:focus-visible {
        outline: 2px solid var(--card-focus);
      }
      svg {
        inline-size: 22px;
        fill: none;
        stroke: currentColor;
        stroke-width: 1.8;
        stroke-linecap: round;
        stroke-linejoin: round;
      }
    }
  }
  @keyframes nudge {
    50% {
      translate: 0 5px;
    }
  }
  #how-text {
    scroll-margin-block-start: 8vmin;
    padding: 26px 30px 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--card-border);
    border-radius: 16px;
    background: var(--card-bg);
    backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    box-shadow: 0 24px 60px -30px var(--card-shadow);
    > p {
      margin-block-end: 18px;
      font-size: 16px;
      line-height: 1.55;
      color: var(--card-lead);
      text-wrap: pretty;
      > a {
        color: var(--card-link);
        text-decoration-color: color-mix(
          in oklch,
          currentColor 40%,
          transparent
        );
        text-underline-offset: 3px;
        &:hover {
          text-decoration-color: currentColor;
        }
      }
    }
    ul {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      list-style: none;
      counter-reset: point;
      border-block-start: 1px solid var(--card-line);
    }
    li {
      counter-increment: point;
      position: relative;
      padding: 13px 0 13px 2.6em;
      text-wrap: pretty;
    }
    li + li {
      border-block-start: 1px solid var(--card-line);
    }
    li::before {
      content: counter(point, decimal-leading-zero);
      position: absolute;
      inset-inline-start: 0;
      font:
        500 12px/2 ui-monospace,
        "SF Mono",
        Menlo,
        monospace;
      color: var(--card-faint);
    }
    code {
      font:
        500 0.88em ui-monospace,
        "SF Mono",
        Menlo,
        monospace;
      color: var(--card-code);
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    a:has(> code) {
      padding: 1px 6px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      background: var(--card-chip);
      box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--card-border);
      text-decoration: none;
      transition: background 150ms;
      &:hover {
        background: var(--card-chip-hover);
      }
    }
    a:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--card-focus);
      outline-offset: 2px;
      border-radius: 6px;
    }
  }
  /* The labels in the corners, as on Hive's pen: Reactive CSS on the right, the X profile on the left. */
  .labels {
    position: fixed;
    inset: auto 14px 14px auto;
    z-index: 5;
    display: flex;
    gap: 8px;
    font:
      600 12px/1 system-ui,
      sans-serif;
  }
  .labels > * {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    text-decoration: none;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 14px var(--pill-shadow);
  }
  .label-made {
    background: var(--pill-bg);
    color: var(--pill-ink);
    border: 1px solid var(--pill-border);
    b {
      color: var(--pill-strong);
    }
  }
  :is(.label-link, .label-x) {
    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--card-focus);
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }
  .label-link {
    background: var(--site-bg);
    color: var(--site-ink);
    &:hover {
      background: var(--site-hover);
    }
  }
  .labels.left {
    inset: auto auto 14px 14px;
  }
  .label-x {
    background: var(--pill-bg);
    color: var(--pill-strong);
    border: 1px solid var(--pill-border);
    &:hover {
      background: var(--pill-hover);
    }
  }
  /* Phones: the labels end the page instead of floating over the piece. */
  @media (width < 700px) {
    .labels {
      position: static;
      justify-content: center;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      padding-block-end: 3vmin;
    }
    .labels.left {
      padding-block-end: 6vmin;
    }
  }
  /* The piece fills the first screen. "How it is built" and an arrow sit at the bottom of that screen
     (pulled up by the height of that head), and the explanation follows below. */
  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
  }
  main {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 100dvh auto;
    justify-items: center;
  }
  .colophon {
    --head: 104px;
    margin-block-start: calc(-1 * var(--head));
    padding-block-end: calc(8vmin + 52px);
  }
  .colophon-head {
    block-size: var(--head);
    align-content: end;
    padding-block-end: 18px;
    margin-block-end: 6vmin;
  }
  @media (width < 700px) {
    .colophon {
      padding-block-end: 6vmin;
    }
  }

  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .counter {
      transition: none;
    }
  }
}

/* ---- Reactive CSS ---- */
@layer reactive {
  .counter {
    --n: state(97);
  }
  .counter input[name] {
    bind: --n;
  } /* the hidden field holds the count, two-way */
  /* A click steps once, unless a hold already stepped (the click that ends a hold is skipped). */
  .minus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(max(0, var(--n) - 1));
  }
  .plus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(min(999, var(--n) + 1));
  }

  /* Hold to repeat. --tick goes 0 (the first, long wait), then 1, 2, 1, 2…: each value picks other @keyframes. */
  .step {
    --held: state(0);
    --reps: state(0);
    --tick: state(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointerdown) {
    --held: set(1);
    --reps: set(0);
    --tick: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointerup) {
    --held: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointerleave) {
    --held: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointercancel) {
    --held: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(keydown) {
    --reps: set(0);
  } /* so Enter and Space after a hold still click */
  .step:when(--held == 1) {
    animation: rc-wait 400ms;
  }
  .step:when(--held == 1):when(--tick == 1) {
    animation: rc-a 80ms;
  }
  .step:when(--held == 1):when(--tick == 2) {
    animation: rc-b 80ms;
  }
  .minus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(max(0, var(--n) - 1));
    --reps: set(var(--reps) + 1);
    --tick: set(1 + max(0, 1 - abs(var(--tick) - 1)));
  }
  .plus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(min(999, var(--n) + 1));
    --reps: set(var(--reps) + 1);
    --tick: set(1 + max(0, 1 - abs(var(--tick) - 1)));
  }

  /* At the ends, the button that can't step fades. */
  .counter:when(--n == 0) .minus {
    opacity: 0.35;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .counter:when(--n == 999) .plus {
    opacity: 0.35;
    cursor: default;
  }
}

/* ---- v2: drag the number like a dial, and the hints (inside .v2 and .v3) ---- */

/* Registered, so it computes to px and a drag rule can divide a px distance by it. */
@property --notch {
  syntax: "<length>";
  inherits: true;
  initial-value: 10px;
}

@font-face {
  font-family: "Gochi Hand";
  src: url("https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/gochihand/v27/hES06XlsOjtJsgCkx1Pkfon_-18kTWE.woff2")
    format("woff2");
  font-display: swap;
}

@layer base {
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value {
    position: relative;
    align-self: stretch; /* as tall as the buttons, the digits centred inside */
    align-items: center;
    padding-inline: 16px;
    border-radius: calc(var(--b) * 0.24);
    cursor: ns-resize;
    touch-action: none; /* a vertical drag turns the dial instead of scrolling the page */
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-user-select: none;
    transition: background-color 150ms;
    &:hover {
      background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.03);
    }
  }
  /* The dial's ticks, one each side: a ruler that moves 8px per step of --shown, so it turns with the wheels. */
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::before,
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset-block: 14%;
    inline-size: 6px;
    background: repeating-linear-gradient(
        oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.55) 0 1.5px,
        transparent 1.5px 8px
      )
      0 calc(var(--shown) * -8px) / 100% 8px;
    mask: linear-gradient(transparent, black 30%, black 70%, transparent);
    opacity: 0.3;
    transition: opacity 200ms;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::before {
    inset-inline-start: 4px;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::after {
    inset-inline-end: 4px;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:hover::before,
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:hover::after {
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  :is(.v2, .v3) .hint {
    position: relative;
    color: var(--hint, oklch(60% 0 0));
    font:
      400 clamp(24px, 4.2vmin, 36px)/1 "Gochi Hand",
      "Comic Sans MS",
      cursive;
    pointer-events: none;
    transition:
      opacity 500ms,
      translate 500ms;
    svg {
      position: absolute;
      overflow: visible;
      fill: none;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 2.4px;
      stroke-linecap: round;
      stroke-linejoin: round;
      animation: hint-bob 2.4s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
    .label {
      display: inline-block;
      max-inline-size: 7em;
      text-wrap: balance;
      rotate: var(--tilt, -4deg);
      text-shadow: var(--hint-shadow, 0 2px 12px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.85));
    }
    path {
      vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke;
    }
    .on-touch {
      display: none;
    }
    @media (hover: none) {
      .on-hover {
        display: none;
      }
      .on-touch {
        display: inline;
      }
    }
  }
  @keyframes hint-bob {
    50% {
      translate: var(--bob, 0 -4px);
    }
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .hint svg {
      animation: none;
    }
  }

  /* Placement, around the counter: "drag" above the dial, the arrow falling into it; "click" and "press" below the
     buttons, their arrows rising into − and + (the "press" arrow mirrored). */
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter {
    position: relative;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .hint {
    --hint: oklch(55% 0 0);
    position: absolute;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .h-drag {
    --bob: 2px 4px;
    --tilt: -3deg;
    inset: auto 0 calc(100% + 50px);
    inline-size: fit-content;
    margin-inline: auto;
    svg {
      inset: calc(100% + 4px) auto auto 45%;
      block-size: 38px;
      scale: 1 -1;
    }
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .h-click {
    --bob: 2px -4px;
    inset: calc(100% + 44px) calc(100% - 8px - var(--b) / 2 + 4px) auto auto;
    svg {
      inset: auto -18px calc(100% + 4px) auto;
      block-size: 36px;
    }
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .h-press {
    --bob: -2px -4px;
    --tilt: 4deg;
    inset: calc(100% + 44px) auto auto calc(100% - 8px - var(--b) / 2 + 4px);
    svg {
      inset: auto auto calc(100% + 4px) -18px;
      block-size: 36px;
      scale: -1 1;
    }
  }
}

@layer reactive {
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter {
    --base: state(97);
    --dragging: state(0);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag(start)) {
    --base: set(var(--n));
    --dragging: set(1);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag) {
    --n: set(clamp(0, round(var(--base) - event(delta-y) / var(--notch)), 999));
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag(end)) {
    --dragging: set(0);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag(cancel)) {
    --dragging: set(0);
  }

  /* Each hint fades after its first real use: a drag move, a click step, a hold repeat. */
  :root {
    --tried-drag: state(false);
    --tried-click: state(false);
    --tried-press: state(false);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag) {
    --tried-drag: set(true);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .step:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --tried-click: set(true);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .step:on(animationend) {
    --tried-press: set(true);
  }
  :root:when(--tried-drag) :is(.v2, .v3) .h-drag {
    opacity: 0;
    translate: 0 -8px;
  }
  :root:when(--tried-click) :is(.v2, .v3) .h-click {
    opacity: 0;
    translate: 0 8px;
  }
  :root:when(--tried-press) :is(.v2, .v3) .h-press {
    opacity: 0;
    translate: 0 8px;
  }

  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) {
    transition: --shown 180ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) .value {
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.05);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) .value::before,
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) .value::after {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

/* ---- v3: the form sets the range and the step; a disabled field stops the counter (inside .v3) ---- */

@layer base {
  /* Before the runtime starts, the count is the form's 95, not v1's 97. */
  .v3 .counter {
    --shown: var(--n, 95);
    --blur: min(
      1,
      max(var(--n, 95) - var(--shown), var(--shown) - var(--n, 95))
    );
  }
  .v3 .sr::after {
    counter-reset: n var(--n, 95);
  }

  /* At an end, the button that can't step fades: 1 when the count is at the limit, 0 a step or more away. */
  .v3 .minus {
    opacity: calc(
      1 - 0.65 * clamp(0, (var(--lo, 0) - var(--n, 95)) / var(--step, 5) + 1, 1)
    );
  }
  .v3 .plus {
    opacity: calc(
      1 - 0.65 *
        clamp(0, (var(--n, 95) - var(--hi, 150)) / var(--step, 5) + 1, 1)
    );
  }

  /* Disabled field: the counter dims and takes no input. */
  .v3 .counter:has(input[name]:disabled) {
    .step,
    .value {
      opacity: 0.35;
      pointer-events: none;
    }
    .hint {
      opacity: 0;
    }
  }

  /* Demo: the checkbox and the field as the form sees it, under the counter. */
  .v3 .bar {
    position: absolute;
    inset: calc(100% + 120px) 0 auto;
    display: grid;
    justify-items: center;
    gap: 14px;
    color: oklch(62% 0 0);
    font:
      400 14px/1.4 system-ui,
      -apple-system,
      "Segoe UI",
      sans-serif;
  }
  .v3 .toggle {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    cursor: pointer;
    input {
      inline-size: 16px;
      block-size: 16px;
      margin: 0;
      accent-color: oklch(80% 0 0);
    }
  }
  .v3 .field {
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    background: oklch(23.5% 0 0);
    color: oklch(72% 0 0);
    font:
      12px/1.4 ui-monospace,
      "SF Mono",
      Menlo,
      monospace;
    text-align: center;
    counter-reset: v var(--n, 95) lo var(--lo, 0) hi var(--hi, 150) st
      var(--step, 5);
    &::before {
      content: '<input name="quantity" value="' counter(v) '" min="' counter(lo)
        '" max="' counter(hi) '" step="' counter(st) '"';
    }
    &::after {
      content: ">";
    }
  }
  .v3 .counter:has(input[name]:disabled) .field::after {
    content: " disabled>";
    color: oklch(80% 0.1 30);
  }
}

@layer reactive {
  /* shortcut: data-max should be data-min plus a whole number of steps; otherwise the top clamp lands off the step. */
  .v3 .counter {
    --n: state(attr(data-value, 97));
    --lo: state(attr(data-min, 0));
    --hi: state(attr(data-max, 999));
    --step: state(attr(data-step, 1));
    --off: state(false);
  }

  /* The same selectors as in style.css and v2.css, so these replace the 0–999, step-1 rules. */
  .v3 .minus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(max(var(--lo), var(--n) - var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .plus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(min(var(--hi), var(--n) + var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .minus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(max(var(--lo), var(--n) - var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .plus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(min(var(--hi), var(--n) + var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .value:on(drag) {
    --n: set(
      clamp(
        var(--lo),
        var(--base) - round(event(delta-y) / var(--notch)) * var(--step),
        var(--hi)
      )
    );
  }

  /* The checkbox disables the field; with it off there is no declaration, so the field's own attribute comes back. */
  .v3 .counter input[type="checkbox"] {
    bind: --off;
  }
  .v3 .counter:when(--off) input[name] {
    --attr-disabled: true;
  }
  /* A disabled field disables the buttons, so they leave the Tab order and ignore keys. */
  .v3 .counter:has(input[name]:disabled) .step {
    --attr-disabled: true;
  }
}

@layer reactive {
  .counter {
    --dragging: state(0);
    --off: state(false);
  }
}

/* ---- The versions: labelled tabs (radio buttons) at the top of the first screen, and a line saying what the open one
   adds. The open version's counter is shown, the others are hidden. ---- */
.versions {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 20px 16px auto;
  z-index: 10;
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  text-align: center;
  font:
    13px/1.4 system-ui,
    -apple-system,
    "Segoe UI",
    sans-serif;
}
.tabs {
  display: inline-flex;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 3px;
  margin: 0;
  border: 1px solid oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.14);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: oklch(16% 0 0 / 0.8);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 14px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.25);
  label {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 7px 12px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    color: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.62);
    font-weight: 600;
    white-space: nowrap;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition:
      background 150ms,
      color 150ms;
  }
  b {
    font:
      500 11px/1 ui-monospace,
      "SF Mono",
      Menlo,
      monospace;
    opacity: 0.7;
  }
  label:hover {
    color: white;
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.08);
  }
  label:has(:checked) {
    color: oklch(16% 0 0);
    background: white;
  }
  label:has(:focus-visible) {
    outline: 2px solid oklch(70% 0.12 250);
    outline-offset: 1px;
  }
  input {
    position: absolute;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
}
.about {
  max-inline-size: 34em;
  margin: 0;
  color: oklch(66% 0 0);
  text-wrap: balance;
}
@media (max-width: 420px) {
  .tabs label {
    padding: 7px 9px;
    gap: 4px;
  }
}
.ver {
  display: contents;
}
body:has(#v1:not(:checked)) :is(.v1, .a1),
body:has(#v2:not(:checked)) :is(.v2, .a2),
body:has(#v3:not(:checked)) :is(.v3, .a3) {
  display: none;
}

/* The pens' background: CSS punctuation, scattered and faint. */
body::before {
  content: "";
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: -1;
  pointer-events: none;
  background: var(--pattern-ink, oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.05));
  mask: url("data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A//www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20width%3D%22240%22%20height%3D%22200%22%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2822%2026%29%20rotate%28-8%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M3-10C0-10-.6-8.6-.6-6.4V-2.4C-.6-1.1-1.4-.2-3%200-1.4.2-.6%201.1-.6%202.4V6.4C-.6%208.6%200%2010%203%2010%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2840%2026%29%20rotate%28-8%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-3-10C0-10%20.6-8.6%20.6-6.4V-2.4C.6-1.1%201.4-.2%203%200%201.4.2.6%201.1.6%202.4V6.4C.6%208.6%200%2010-3%2010%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28104%2042%29%20rotate%2810%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%220%22%20cy%3D%22-5%22%20r%3D%221.5%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M.4%203.2C1%204.6.6%206.8-1.4%208.4%22/%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%22.2%22%20cy%3D%223.4%22%20r%3D%221.5%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28168%2022%29%20rotate%286%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-2.5-8-4.5%208M3.5-8%201.5%208M-7-3H7M-8%203H6%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28214%2070%29%20rotate%280%29%20scale%280.8%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M0-7V7M-6-3.5%206%203.5M-6%203.5%206-3.5%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2866%2092%29%20rotate%28-12%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-5-9C-9-4-9%204-5%209M5-9C9-4%209%204%205%209%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28136%20104%29%20rotate%280%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%220%22%20cy%3D%22-4.5%22%20r%3D%221.5%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%220%22%20cy%3D%224.5%22%20r%3D%221.5%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28196%20140%29%20rotate%28-6%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-4-6%204%200-4%206%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2824%20150%29%20rotate%288%29%20scale%280.85%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M6-8-6%208%22/%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%22-4.5%22%20cy%3D%22-5.5%22%20r%3D%222.4%22/%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%224.5%22%20cy%3D%225.5%22%20r%3D%222.4%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28104%20168%29%20rotate%284%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-4-9H-7V9H-4M4-9H7V9H4%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28166%20186%29%20rotate%280%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M4-9-4%209%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28226%20192%29%20rotate%280%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%220%22%20cy%3D%220%22%20r%3D%221.7%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28150%2060%29%20rotate%280%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%220%22%20cy%3D%220%22%20r%3D%221.7%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2854%20196%29%20rotate%2814%29%20scale%280.6%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M0-7V7M-6-3.5%206%203.5M-6%203.5%206-3.5%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3C/svg%3E")
    0 0 / 240px 200px repeat;
}



<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Rolling Counter · Reactive CSS</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
  <script src="https://unpkg.com/@reactivecss/core@0.9.1/dist/reactive-css.min.js" defer></script>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="versions">
    <fieldset class="tabs" aria-label="Version"><label><input type="radio" name="version" id="v1"><b>v1</b>Click &amp; hold</label><label><input type="radio" name="version" id="v2"><b>v2</b>Drag dial</label><label><input type="radio" name="version" id="v3" checked><b>v3</b>Forms</label></fieldset>
    <p class="about a1">Tap − or + to step; hold one to repeat. A hidden field carries the count into a form.</p>
    <p class="about a2">Everything in v1, plus: drag the number up or down like a dial, one step every 10px.</p>
    <p class="about a3">Everything in v2, plus: the form sets the range and step (0–150, by 5), and disabling the field stops the counter.</p>
  </div>
  <main>
    <div class="ver v1">
      <form class="counter" role="group" aria-label="Counter">
        <!-- The form field: kept in step with the count, so the form submits it as quantity=97. -->
        <input type="number" name="quantity" value="97" min="0" max="999" hidden>
        <button type="button" class="step minus" aria-label="Decrease"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14"/></svg></button>
        <span class="value">
          <span class="reel h" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel t" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel u" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span>
          <output class="sr" role="status"></output>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="step plus" aria-label="Increase"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5v14"/></svg></button>
      </form>
    </div>
    <div class="ver v2">
      <form class="counter" role="group" aria-label="Counter">
        <!-- The form field: kept in step with the count, so the form submits it as quantity=97. -->
        <input type="number" name="quantity" value="97" min="0" max="999" hidden>
        <button type="button" class="step minus" aria-label="Decrease"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14"/></svg></button>
        <span class="value" title="Drag up or down">
          <span class="reel h" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel t" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel u" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span>
          <output class="sr" role="status"></output>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="step plus" aria-label="Increase"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5v14"/></svg></button>
        <!-- Hints: one for each way to change the count; each fades after its first real use. -->
        <p class="hint h-drag" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">drag</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-click" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label"><span class="on-hover">click</span><span class="on-touch">tap</span></span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-press" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">press</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
      </form>
    </div>
    <div class="ver v3">
      <!-- The limits are on the form, which owns the count: data-value, data-min, data-max and data-step become its
           starting count, range and step. The field repeats them, so the browser validates it the same way. -->
      <form class="counter" role="group" aria-label="Quantity" data-value="95" data-min="0" data-max="150" data-step="5">
        <input type="number" name="quantity" value="95" min="0" max="150" step="5" hidden>
        <button type="button" class="step minus" aria-label="Decrease"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14"/></svg></button>
        <span class="value" title="Drag up or down">
          <span class="reel h" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel t" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel u" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span>
          <output class="sr" role="status"></output>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="step plus" aria-label="Increase"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5v14"/></svg></button>
        <!-- Hints: one for each way to change the count; each fades after its first real use. -->
        <p class="hint h-drag" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">drag</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-click" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label"><span class="on-hover">click</span><span class="on-touch">tap</span></span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-press" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">press</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <!-- Demo only: the checkbox disables the field, and the line under it shows the field as the form sees it. -->
        <div class="bar">
          <label class="toggle"><input type="checkbox"> Disable the field</label>
          <code class="field" aria-hidden="true"></code>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
    <section class="colophon" aria-labelledby="how">
      <div class="colophon-head"><h2 id="how">How it is built</h2><a class="down" href="#how-text" aria-label="Read how it is built"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 4v15M6 13l6 6 6-6"/></svg></a></div>
      <div id="how-text">
      <p>The reels are plain CSS: each digit is a column 0–9 plus a second 0, moved by <code>mod()</code> and <code>round()</code> of a registered number that eases toward the count, so 99 → 100 turns all three wheels together and leading zeros fold away. <a href="https://reactivecss.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Reactive CSS</a> adds the interaction, with no application JavaScript:</p>
      <ul><li>The count is a <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/state/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>state()</code></a>; − and + are <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/on-rule/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(click)</code></a> rules that <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/set/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>set()</code></a> it within its range. <code>bind:</code> keeps a hidden number field in step with it, so it submits with the form.</li><li>Hold a button to repeat: while it is held, a short animation runs, and each <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/on-rule/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(animationend)</code></a> steps the count and flips a state that swaps the animation, so the next one starts.</li><li>v2: drag the number like a dial. <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/drag/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(drag(start))</code></a> remembers the count, and <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/drag/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(drag)</code></a> sets it from <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/event/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>event(delta-y)</code></a>, one step every 10px.</li><li>v3, for forms: <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/state/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>state(attr(data-max, 999))</code></a> reads the form's range and step as numbers, and <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/attr-property/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>--attr-disabled</code></a> disables the buttons when the field is disabled.</li><li>Each hint fades after its first real use, through a page-wide <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/state/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>state()</code></a> and <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/when-selector/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:when()</code></a>.</li></ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>
  <div class="labels">
    <span class="label-made">Built with <b>Reactive CSS</b></span>
    <a class="label-link" href="https://reactivecss.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener">reactivecss.com ↗</a>
  </div>
  <div class="labels left">
    <a class="label-x" href="https://x.com/troshkin_pavel" target="_blank" rel="noopener">𝕏 @troshkin_pavel</a>
  </div>
</body>
</html>

Rolling Counter: a stepper whose digits roll like an odometer, in three versions (click and hold, a drag dial, forms), with CSS and Reactive CSS.

No application JavaScript. The only script is the Reactive CSS runtime, loaded in the HTML: https://unpkg.com/@reactivecss/core@0.9.1/dist/reactive-css.min.js

Everything the page does is in style.css.
*/

migrate for Tailwind to vanilla + a shadcn project (components.json) to existing non shadcn migration. Install with the Dither Kit CLI (pulls the shared `core` engine + deps like motion and d3 automatically; writes a lockfile so you can update later):

  npx @dither-kit/cli add area-chart

  npx @dither-kit/cli list



Files land in components/dither-kit/. The API is recharts-style / children-as-config — a data array plus a config object that maps each series to a label and colour:



  import { AreaChart, Area, XAxis, YAxis, Legend, Tooltip } from "@/components/dither-kit/area-chart"



  const data = [{ month: "Jan", desktop: 186 }, { month: "Feb", desktop: 240 }]

  const config = { desktop: { label: "Desktop", color: "blue" } }



  <AreaChart data={data} config={config} bloom="aura">

    <XAxis dataKey="month" />

    <YAxis />

    <Legend isClickable />

    <Tooltip labelKey="month" />

    <Area dataKey="desktop" variant="gradient" />

  </AreaChart>



Other charts: bar-chart, pie-chart, radar-chart (or `npx @dither-kit/cli add dither-kit` for everything).

- variant: gradient | dotted | hatched | solid

- color: green blue purple pink orange red grey

- bloom: off | low | high | aura



Three standalone extras (no chart engine, tiny installs — same CLI):

- avatar — generative mirrored pixel avatars: <DitherAvatar name="dan" /> (optional hue={0-360})

- button — dithered native buttons: <DitherButton color="blue" variant="gradient">label</DitherButton>

- gradient — dithered background washes: <DitherGradient from="purple" direction="up" /> inside a relative container

@layer base, reactive;

/* Registered, so it transitions: the count as it is drawn, easing toward the real one. */
@property --shown {
  syntax: "<number>";
  inherits: true;
  initial-value: 97;
}

/* Timers for hold-to-repeat: they change nothing visible, only their animationend counts. */
@keyframes rc-wait {
  to {
    --rc-t: 1;
  }
}
@keyframes rc-a {
  to {
    --rc-t: 1;
  }
}
@keyframes rc-b {
  to {
    --rc-t: 1;
  }
}

@layer base {
  :root {
    color-scheme: dark;
  }
  * {
    box-sizing: border-box;
    margin: 0;
  }

  body {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    min-block-size: 100dvh;
    padding: 24px 16px;
    background: oklch(19.5% 0 0);
    font-family:
      system-ui,
      -apple-system,
      "Segoe UI",
      sans-serif;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  }

  .counter {
    --b: clamp(64px, 17vw, 92px); /* button size */
    --shown: var(--n, 97);
    /* The distance still to go, 0 to 1: the wheels blur by it, so they are sharp at rest and soft when spinning. */
    --blur: min(
      1,
      max(var(--n, 97) - var(--shown), var(--shown) - var(--n, 97))
    );
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: clamp(8px, 2vw, 14px);
    padding: 8px;
    border-radius: calc(var(--b) * 0.32);
    background: oklch(23.5% 0 0);
    box-shadow:
      inset 0 1px 0 oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.04),
      0 10px 30px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.35);
    transition: --shown 650ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
  }

  .step {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    inline-size: var(--b);
    aspect-ratio: 1;
    padding: 0;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: calc(var(--b) * 0.24);
    background: oklch(30% 0 0);
    color: oklch(96% 0 0);
    cursor: pointer;
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-user-select: none;
    -webkit-touch-callout: none; /* a long press on a phone holds to repeat, without the system menu */
    touch-action: manipulation;
    transition:
      background-color 150ms,
      scale 150ms,
      opacity 200ms;
    svg {
      inline-size: 34%;
      fill: none;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 1.8px;
      stroke-linecap: round;
    }
    &:hover {
      background: oklch(34% 0 0);
    }
    &:active {
      scale: 0.94;
    }
    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid oklch(80% 0 0);
      outline-offset: 3px;
    }
  }

  /* The digits: three windows side by side; a hidden one is 0 wide. */
  .value {
    display: flex;
    justify-content: center;
    min-inline-size: calc(var(--b) * 1.2);
    color: oklch(97% 0 0);
    font:
      500 calc(var(--b) * 0.72)/1 system-ui,
      -apple-system,
      "Segoe UI",
      sans-serif;
    font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
    letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  }
  .reel {
    --cell: 1.2em;
    inline-size: calc(0.62em * var(--vis, 1));
    block-size: var(--cell);
    overflow: hidden;
    opacity: var(--vis, 1);
    mask: linear-gradient(
      transparent,
      black 22%,
      black 78%,
      transparent
    ); /* digits fade in and out at the edges */
  }
  .strip {
    display: grid;
    justify-items: center;
    translate: 0 calc(var(--d)  *-1*  var(--cell));
    filter: blur(calc(var(--blur) * 1.4px));
    i {
      block-size: var(--cell);
      line-height: var(--cell);
      font-style: normal;
    }
  }
  /* Each wheel's position, from --shown: units turn all the time; tens and hundreds only on the carry (the last step
     of the wheel below, from 9 to 0). Leading wheels fold away below 10 and 100. */
  .u {
    --d: mod(var(--shown), 10);
  }
  .t {
    --d: mod(
      round(down, var(--shown) / 10) + clamp(0, mod(var(--shown), 10) - 9, 1),
      10
    );
    --vis: clamp(0, var(--shown) - 9, 1);
  }
  .h {
    --d: mod(
      round(down, var(--shown) / 100) + clamp(0, mod(var(--shown), 100) - 99, 1),
      10
    );
    --vis: clamp(0, var(--shown) - 99, 1);
  }

  /* For screen readers: the count as text, announced when it changes. */
  .sr {
    position: absolute;
    inline-size: 1px;
    block-size: 1px;
    overflow: hidden;
    clip-path: inset(50%);
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .sr::after {
    counter-reset: n var(--n, 97);
    content: counter(n);
  }

  body {
    display: block;
    padding: 0 16px;
  }
  .counter {
    align-self: center;
  }
  .colophon,
  .labels {
    --card-heading: oklch(88% 0 0);
    --card-lead: oklch(86% 0 0);
    --card-text: oklch(64% 0 0);
    --card-faint: oklch(50% 0 0);
    --card-code: oklch(90% 0 0);
    --card-link: white;
    --card-bg: oklch(18% 0 0 / 0.72);
    --card-border: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.1);
    --card-line: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.07);
    --card-chip: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.07);
    --card-chip-hover: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.16);
    --card-shadow: oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.8);
    --card-focus: oklch(80% 0 0);
    --pill-bg: oklch(22% 0 0);
    --pill-ink: oklch(72% 0 0);
    --pill-strong: white;
    --pill-border: oklch(32% 0 0);
    --pill-hover: oklch(30% 0 0);
    --site-bg: oklch(94% 0 0);
    --site-ink: oklch(20% 0 0);
    --site-hover: white;
    --pill-shadow: oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.5);
  }
  /* The explanation: a card set apart from the piece. A lead sentence, then numbered points; the functions are chips
     that link to their reference pages, so it reads as notes about the page, not part of it. */
  .colophon {
    inline-size: min(100%, 680px);
    font:
      14px/1.6 system-ui,
      sans-serif;
    color: var(--card-text);
  }
  .colophon-head {
    display: grid;
    justify-items: center;
    gap: 4px;
    h2 {
      font:
        650 15px/1.3 system-ui,
        sans-serif;
      letter-spacing: -0.01em;
      color: var(--card-heading);
    }
    .down {
      display: grid;
      place-items: center;
      inline-size: 40px;
      block-size: 40px;
      border-radius: 50%;
      color: var(--card-faint);
      animation: nudge 2.2s ease-in-out infinite;
      &:hover {
        color: var(--card-link);
      }
      &:focus-visible {
        outline: 2px solid var(--card-focus);
      }
      svg {
        inline-size: 22px;
        fill: none;
        stroke: currentColor;
        stroke-width: 1.8;
        stroke-linecap: round;
        stroke-linejoin: round;
      }
    }
  }
  @keyframes nudge {
    50% {
      translate: 0 5px;
    }
  }
  #how-text {
    scroll-margin-block-start: 8vmin;
    padding: 26px 30px 10px;
    border: 1px solid var(--card-border);
    border-radius: 16px;
    background: var(--card-bg);
    backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
    box-shadow: 0 24px 60px -30px var(--card-shadow);
    > p {
      margin-block-end: 18px;
      font-size: 16px;
      line-height: 1.55;
      color: var(--card-lead);
      text-wrap: pretty;
      > a {
        color: var(--card-link);
        text-decoration-color: color-mix(
          in oklch,
          currentColor 40%,
          transparent
        );
        text-underline-offset: 3px;
        &:hover {
          text-decoration-color: currentColor;
        }
      }
    }
    ul {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      list-style: none;
      counter-reset: point;
      border-block-start: 1px solid var(--card-line);
    }
    li {
      counter-increment: point;
      position: relative;
      padding: 13px 0 13px 2.6em;
      text-wrap: pretty;
    }
    li + li {
      border-block-start: 1px solid var(--card-line);
    }
    li::before {
      content: counter(point, decimal-leading-zero);
      position: absolute;
      inset-inline-start: 0;
      font:
        500 12px/2 ui-monospace,
        "SF Mono",
        Menlo,
        monospace;
      color: var(--card-faint);
    }
    code {
      font:
        500 0.88em ui-monospace,
        "SF Mono",
        Menlo,
        monospace;
      color: var(--card-code);
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    a:has(> code) {
      padding: 1px 6px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      background: var(--card-chip);
      box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--card-border);
      text-decoration: none;
      transition: background 150ms;
      &:hover {
        background: var(--card-chip-hover);
      }
    }
    a:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--card-focus);
      outline-offset: 2px;
      border-radius: 6px;
    }
  }
  /* The labels in the corners, as on Hive's pen: Reactive CSS on the right, the X profile on the left. */
  .labels {
    position: fixed;
    inset: auto 14px 14px auto;
    z-index: 5;
    display: flex;
    gap: 8px;
    font:
      600 12px/1 system-ui,
      sans-serif;
  }
  .labels > * {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    text-decoration: none;
    box-shadow: 0 4px 14px var(--pill-shadow);
  }
  .label-made {
    background: var(--pill-bg);
    color: var(--pill-ink);
    border: 1px solid var(--pill-border);
    b {
      color: var(--pill-strong);
    }
  }
  :is(.label-link, .label-x) {
    &:focus-visible {
      outline: 2px solid var(--card-focus);
      outline-offset: 2px;
    }
  }
  .label-link {
    background: var(--site-bg);
    color: var(--site-ink);
    &:hover {
      background: var(--site-hover);
    }
  }
  .labels.left {
    inset: auto auto 14px 14px;
  }
  .label-x {
    background: var(--pill-bg);
    color: var(--pill-strong);
    border: 1px solid var(--pill-border);
    &:hover {
      background: var(--pill-hover);
    }
  }
  /* Phones: the labels end the page instead of floating over the piece. */
  @media (width < 700px) {
    .labels {
      position: static;
      justify-content: center;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
      padding-block-end: 3vmin;
    }
    .labels.left {
      padding-block-end: 6vmin;
    }
  }
  /* The piece fills the first screen. "How it is built" and an arrow sit at the bottom of that screen
     (pulled up by the height of that head), and the explanation follows below. */
  html {
    scroll-behavior: smooth;
  }
  main {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-rows: 100dvh auto;
    justify-items: center;
  }
  .colophon {
    --head: 104px;
    margin-block-start: calc(-1 * var(--head));
    padding-block-end: calc(8vmin + 52px);
  }
  .colophon-head {
    block-size: var(--head);
    align-content: end;
    padding-block-end: 18px;
    margin-block-end: 6vmin;
  }
  @media (width < 700px) {
    .colophon {
      padding-block-end: 6vmin;
    }
  }

  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .counter {
      transition: none;
    }
  }
}

/* ---- Reactive CSS ---- */
@layer reactive {
  .counter {
    --n: state(97);
  }
  .counter input[name] {
    bind: --n;
  } /* the hidden field holds the count, two-way */
  /* A click steps once, unless a hold already stepped (the click that ends a hold is skipped). */
  .minus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(max(0, var(--n) - 1));
  }
  .plus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(min(999, var(--n) + 1));
  }

  /* Hold to repeat. --tick goes 0 (the first, long wait), then 1, 2, 1, 2…: each value picks other @keyframes. */
  .step {
    --held: state(0);
    --reps: state(0);
    --tick: state(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointerdown) {
    --held: set(1);
    --reps: set(0);
    --tick: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointerup) {
    --held: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointerleave) {
    --held: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(pointercancel) {
    --held: set(0);
  }
  .step:on(keydown) {
    --reps: set(0);
  } /* so Enter and Space after a hold still click */
  .step:when(--held == 1) {
    animation: rc-wait 400ms;
  }
  .step:when(--held == 1):when(--tick == 1) {
    animation: rc-a 80ms;
  }
  .step:when(--held == 1):when(--tick == 2) {
    animation: rc-b 80ms;
  }
  .minus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(max(0, var(--n) - 1));
    --reps: set(var(--reps) + 1);
    --tick: set(1 + max(0, 1 - abs(var(--tick) - 1)));
  }
  .plus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(min(999, var(--n) + 1));
    --reps: set(var(--reps) + 1);
    --tick: set(1 + max(0, 1 - abs(var(--tick) - 1)));
  }

  /* At the ends, the button that can't step fades. */
  .counter:when(--n == 0) .minus {
    opacity: 0.35;
    cursor: default;
  }
  .counter:when(--n == 999) .plus {
    opacity: 0.35;
    cursor: default;
  }
}

/* ---- v2: drag the number like a dial, and the hints (inside .v2 and .v3) ---- */

/* Registered, so it computes to px and a drag rule can divide a px distance by it. */
@property --notch {
  syntax: "<length>";
  inherits: true;
  initial-value: 10px;
}

@font-face {
  font-family: "Gochi Hand";
  src: url("https://fonts.gstatic.com/s/gochihand/v27/hES06XlsOjtJsgCkx1Pkfon_-18kTWE.woff2")
    format("woff2");
  font-display: swap;
}

@layer base {
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value {
    position: relative;
    align-self: stretch; /* as tall as the buttons, the digits centred inside */
    align-items: center;
    padding-inline: 16px;
    border-radius: calc(var(--b) * 0.24);
    cursor: ns-resize;
    touch-action: none; /* a vertical drag turns the dial instead of scrolling the page */
    user-select: none;
    -webkit-user-select: none;
    transition: background-color 150ms;
    &:hover {
      background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.03);
    }
  }
  /* The dial's ticks, one each side: a ruler that moves 8px per step of --shown, so it turns with the wheels. */
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::before,
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::after {
    content: "";
    position: absolute;
    inset-block: 14%;
    inline-size: 6px;
    background: repeating-linear-gradient(
        oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.55) 0 1.5px,
        transparent 1.5px 8px
      )
      0 calc(var(--shown) * -8px) / 100% 8px;
    mask: linear-gradient(transparent, black 30%, black 70%, transparent);
    opacity: 0.3;
    transition: opacity 200ms;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::before {
    inset-inline-start: 4px;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value::after {
    inset-inline-end: 4px;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:hover::before,
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:hover::after {
    opacity: 0.7;
  }

  :is(.v2, .v3) .hint {
    position: relative;
    color: var(--hint, oklch(60% 0 0));
    font:
      400 clamp(24px, 4.2vmin, 36px)/1 "Gochi Hand",
      "Comic Sans MS",
      cursive;
    pointer-events: none;
    transition:
      opacity 500ms,
      translate 500ms;
    svg {
      position: absolute;
      overflow: visible;
      fill: none;
      stroke: currentColor;
      stroke-width: 2.4px;
      stroke-linecap: round;
      stroke-linejoin: round;
      animation: hint-bob 2.4s ease-in-out infinite;
    }
    .label {
      display: inline-block;
      max-inline-size: 7em;
      text-wrap: balance;
      rotate: var(--tilt, -4deg);
      text-shadow: var(--hint-shadow, 0 2px 12px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.85));
    }
    path {
      vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke;
    }
    .on-touch {
      display: none;
    }
    @media (hover: none) {
      .on-hover {
        display: none;
      }
      .on-touch {
        display: inline;
      }
    }
  }
  @keyframes hint-bob {
    50% {
      translate: var(--bob, 0 -4px);
    }
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .hint svg {
      animation: none;
    }
  }

  /* Placement, around the counter: "drag" above the dial, the arrow falling into it; "click" and "press" below the
     buttons, their arrows rising into − and + (the "press" arrow mirrored). */
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter {
    position: relative;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .hint {
    --hint: oklch(55% 0 0);
    position: absolute;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .h-drag {
    --bob: 2px 4px;
    --tilt: -3deg;
    inset: auto 0 calc(100% + 50px);
    inline-size: fit-content;
    margin-inline: auto;
    svg {
      inset: calc(100% + 4px) auto auto 45%;
      block-size: 38px;
      scale: 1 -1;
    }
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .h-click {
    --bob: 2px -4px;
    inset: calc(100% + 44px) calc(100% - 8px - var(--b) / 2 + 4px) auto auto;
    svg {
      inset: auto -18px calc(100% + 4px) auto;
      block-size: 36px;
    }
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .h-press {
    --bob: -2px -4px;
    --tilt: 4deg;
    inset: calc(100% + 44px) auto auto calc(100% - 8px - var(--b) / 2 + 4px);
    svg {
      inset: auto auto calc(100% + 4px) -18px;
      block-size: 36px;
      scale: -1 1;
    }
  }
}

@layer reactive {
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter {
    --base: state(97);
    --dragging: state(0);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag(start)) {
    --base: set(var(--n));
    --dragging: set(1);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag) {
    --n: set(clamp(0, round(var(--base) - event(delta-y) / var(--notch)), 999));
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag(end)) {
    --dragging: set(0);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag(cancel)) {
    --dragging: set(0);
  }

  /* Each hint fades after its first real use: a drag move, a click step, a hold repeat. */
  :root {
    --tried-drag: state(false);
    --tried-click: state(false);
    --tried-press: state(false);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .value:on(drag) {
    --tried-drag: set(true);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .step:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --tried-click: set(true);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .step:on(animationend) {
    --tried-press: set(true);
  }
  :root:when(--tried-drag) :is(.v2, .v3) .h-drag {
    opacity: 0;
    translate: 0 -8px;
  }
  :root:when(--tried-click) :is(.v2, .v3) .h-click {
    opacity: 0;
    translate: 0 8px;
  }
  :root:when(--tried-press) :is(.v2, .v3) .h-press {
    opacity: 0;
    translate: 0 8px;
  }

  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) {
    transition: --shown 180ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) .value {
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.05);
  }
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) .value::before,
  :is(.v2, .v3) .counter:when(--dragging == 1) .value::after {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

/* ---- v3: the form sets the range and the step; a disabled field stops the counter (inside .v3) ---- */

@layer base {
  /* Before the runtime starts, the count is the form's 95, not v1's 97. */
  .v3 .counter {
    --shown: var(--n, 95);
    --blur: min(
      1,
      max(var(--n, 95) - var(--shown), var(--shown) - var(--n, 95))
    );
  }
  .v3 .sr::after {
    counter-reset: n var(--n, 95);
  }

  /* At an end, the button that can't step fades: 1 when the count is at the limit, 0 a step or more away. */
  .v3 .minus {
    opacity: calc(
      1 - 0.65 * clamp(0, (var(--lo, 0) - var(--n, 95)) / var(--step, 5) + 1, 1)
    );
  }
  .v3 .plus {
    opacity: calc(
      1 - 0.65 *
        clamp(0, (var(--n, 95) - var(--hi, 150)) / var(--step, 5) + 1, 1)
    );
  }

  /* Disabled field: the counter dims and takes no input. */
  .v3 .counter:has(input[name]:disabled) {
    .step,
    .value {
      opacity: 0.35;
      pointer-events: none;
    }
    .hint {
      opacity: 0;
    }
  }

  /* Demo: the checkbox and the field as the form sees it, under the counter. */
  .v3 .bar {
    position: absolute;
    inset: calc(100% + 120px) 0 auto;
    display: grid;
    justify-items: center;
    gap: 14px;
    color: oklch(62% 0 0);
    font:
      400 14px/1.4 system-ui,
      -apple-system,
      "Segoe UI",
      sans-serif;
  }
  .v3 .toggle {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 8px;
    cursor: pointer;
    input {
      inline-size: 16px;
      block-size: 16px;
      margin: 0;
      accent-color: oklch(80% 0 0);
    }
  }
  .v3 .field {
    padding: 8px 12px;
    border-radius: 8px;
    background: oklch(23.5% 0 0);
    color: oklch(72% 0 0);
    font:
      12px/1.4 ui-monospace,
      "SF Mono",
      Menlo,
      monospace;
    text-align: center;
    counter-reset: v var(--n, 95) lo var(--lo, 0) hi var(--hi, 150) st
      var(--step, 5);
    &::before {
      content: '<input name="quantity" value="' counter(v) '" min="' counter(lo)
        '" max="' counter(hi) '" step="' counter(st) '"';
    }
    &::after {
      content: ">";
    }
  }
  .v3 .counter:has(input[name]:disabled) .field::after {
    content: " disabled>";
    color: oklch(80% 0.1 30);
  }
}

@layer reactive {
  /* shortcut: data-max should be data-min plus a whole number of steps; otherwise the top clamp lands off the step. */
  .v3 .counter {
    --n: state(attr(data-value, 97));
    --lo: state(attr(data-min, 0));
    --hi: state(attr(data-max, 999));
    --step: state(attr(data-step, 1));
    --off: state(false);
  }

  /* The same selectors as in style.css and v2.css, so these replace the 0–999, step-1 rules. */
  .v3 .minus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(max(var(--lo), var(--n) - var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .plus:when(--reps == 0):on(click) {
    --n: set(min(var(--hi), var(--n) + var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .minus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(max(var(--lo), var(--n) - var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .plus:on(animationend) {
    --n: set(min(var(--hi), var(--n) + var(--step)));
  }
  .v3 .value:on(drag) {
    --n: set(
      clamp(
        var(--lo),
        var(--base) - round(event(delta-y) / var(--notch)) * var(--step),
        var(--hi)
      )
    );
  }

  /* The checkbox disables the field; with it off there is no declaration, so the field's own attribute comes back. */
  .v3 .counter input[type="checkbox"] {
    bind: --off;
  }
  .v3 .counter:when(--off) input[name] {
    --attr-disabled: true;
  }
  /* A disabled field disables the buttons, so they leave the Tab order and ignore keys. */
  .v3 .counter:has(input[name]:disabled) .step {
    --attr-disabled: true;
  }
}

@layer reactive {
  .counter {
    --dragging: state(0);
    --off: state(false);
  }
}

/* ---- The versions: labelled tabs (radio buttons) at the top of the first screen, and a line saying what the open one
   adds. The open version's counter is shown, the others are hidden. ---- */
.versions {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 20px 16px auto;
  z-index: 10;
  display: grid;
  justify-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  text-align: center;
  font:
    13px/1.4 system-ui,
    -apple-system,
    "Segoe UI",
    sans-serif;
}
.tabs {
  display: inline-flex;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 3px;
  margin: 0;
  border: 1px solid oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.14);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: oklch(16% 0 0 / 0.8);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 14px oklch(0% 0 0 / 0.25);
  label {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 7px 12px;
    border-radius: 999px;
    color: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.62);
    font-weight: 600;
    white-space: nowrap;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition:
      background 150ms,
      color 150ms;
  }
  b {
    font:
      500 11px/1 ui-monospace,
      "SF Mono",
      Menlo,
      monospace;
    opacity: 0.7;
  }
  label:hover {
    color: white;
    background: oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.08);
  }
  label:has(:checked) {
    color: oklch(16% 0 0);
    background: white;
  }
  label:has(:focus-visible) {
    outline: 2px solid oklch(70% 0.12 250);
    outline-offset: 1px;
  }
  input {
    position: absolute;
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
  }
}
.about {
  max-inline-size: 34em;
  margin: 0;
  color: oklch(66% 0 0);
  text-wrap: balance;
}
@media (max-width: 420px) {
  .tabs label {
    padding: 7px 9px;
    gap: 4px;
  }
}
.ver {
  display: contents;
}
body:has(#v1:not(:checked)) :is(.v1, .a1),
body:has(#v2:not(:checked)) :is(.v2, .a2),
body:has(#v3:not(:checked)) :is(.v3, .a3) {
  display: none;
}

/* The pens' background: CSS punctuation, scattered and faint. */
body::before {
  content: "";
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: -1;
  pointer-events: none;
  background: var(--pattern-ink, oklch(100% 0 0 / 0.05));
  mask: url("data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A//www.w3.org/2000/svg%22%20width%3D%22240%22%20height%3D%22200%22%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2822%2026%29%20rotate%28-8%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M3-10C0-10-.6-8.6-.6-6.4V-2.4C-.6-1.1-1.4-.2-3%200-1.4.2-.6%201.1-.6%202.4V6.4C-.6%208.6%200%2010%203%2010%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2840%2026%29%20rotate%28-8%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-3-10C0-10%20.6-8.6%20.6-6.4V-2.4C.6-1.1%201.4-.2%203%200%201.4.2.6%201.1.6%202.4V6.4C.6%208.6%200%2010-3%2010%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28104%2042%29%20rotate%2810%29%20scale%281%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%220%22%20cy%3D%22-5%22%20r%3D%221.5%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M.4%203.2C1%204.6.6%206.8-1.4%208.4%22/%3E%3Ccircle%20cx%3D%22.2%22%20cy%3D%223.4%22%20r%3D%221.5%22%20fill%3D%22black%22%20stroke%3D%22none%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28168%2022%29%20rotate%286%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M-2.5-8-4.5%208M3.5-8%201.5%208M-7-3H7M-8%203H6%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%28214%2070%29%20rotate%280%29%20scale%280.8%29%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%20stroke%3D%22black%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.5%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%3E%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M0-7V7M-6-3.5%206%203.5M-6%203.5%206-3.5%22/%3E%3C/g%3E%3Cg%20transform%3D%22translate%2866%2092%29%20rotate%28-12%29%20scale%280.9%29%22%20fill%3D%2 <!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Rolling Counter · Reactive CSS</title>
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="style.css">
  <script src="https://unpkg.com/@reactivecss/core@0.9.1/dist/reactive-css.min.js" defer></script>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="versions">
    <fieldset class="tabs" aria-label="Version"><label><input type="radio" name="version" id="v1"><b>v1</b>Click &amp; hold</label><label><input type="radio" name="version" id="v2"><b>v2</b>Drag dial</label><label><input type="radio" name="version" id="v3" checked><b>v3</b>Forms</label></fieldset>
    <p class="about a1">Tap − or + to step; hold one to repeat. A hidden field carries the count into a form.</p>
    <p class="about a2">Everything in v1, plus: drag the number up or down like a dial, one step every 10px.</p>
    <p class="about a3">Everything in v2, plus: the form sets the range and step (0–150, by 5), and disabling the field stops the counter.</p>
  </div>
  <main>
    <div class="ver v1">
      <form class="counter" role="group" aria-label="Counter">
        <!-- The form field: kept in step with the count, so the form submits it as quantity=97. -->
        <input type="number" name="quantity" value="97" min="0" max="999" hidden>
        <button type="button" class="step minus" aria-label="Decrease"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14"/></svg></button>
        <span class="value">
          <span class="reel h" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel t" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel u" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span>
          <output class="sr" role="status"></output>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="step plus" aria-label="Increase"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5v14"/></svg></button>
      </form>
    </div>
    <div class="ver v2">
      <form class="counter" role="group" aria-label="Counter">
        <!-- The form field: kept in step with the count, so the form submits it as quantity=97. -->
        <input type="number" name="quantity" value="97" min="0" max="999" hidden>
        <button type="button" class="step minus" aria-label="Decrease"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14"/></svg></button>
        <span class="value" title="Drag up or down">
          <span class="reel h" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel t" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel u" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span>
          <output class="sr" role="status"></output>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="step plus" aria-label="Increase"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5v14"/></svg></button>
        <!-- Hints: one for each way to change the count; each fades after its first real use. -->
        <p class="hint h-drag" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">drag</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-click" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label"><span class="on-hover">click</span><span class="on-touch">tap</span></span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-press" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">press</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
      </form>
    </div>
    <div class="ver v3">
      <!-- The limits are on the form, which owns the count: data-value, data-min, data-max and data-step become its
           starting count, range and step. The field repeats them, so the browser validates it the same way. -->
      <form class="counter" role="group" aria-label="Quantity" data-value="95" data-min="0" data-max="150" data-step="5">
        <input type="number" name="quantity" value="95" min="0" max="150" step="5" hidden>
        <button type="button" class="step minus" aria-label="Decrease"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14"/></svg></button>
        <span class="value" title="Drag up or down">
          <span class="reel h" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel t" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span><span class="reel u" aria-hidden="true"><span class="strip"><i>0</i><i>1</i><i>2</i><i>3</i><i>4</i><i>5</i><i>6</i><i>7</i><i>8</i><i>9</i><i>0</i></span></span>
          <output class="sr" role="status"></output>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="step plus" aria-label="Increase"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5v14"/></svg></button>
        <!-- Hints: one for each way to change the count; each fades after its first real use. -->
        <p class="hint h-drag" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">drag</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-click" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label"><span class="on-hover">click</span><span class="on-touch">tap</span></span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <p class="hint h-press" aria-hidden="true"><span class="label">press</span><svg viewBox="0 0 50 100"><path d="M2 98 C 1 60, 18 24, 48 3 M33 5 L49 2 L45 19"/></svg></p>
        <!-- Demo only: the checkbox disables the field, and the line under it shows the field as the form sees it. -->
        <div class="bar">
          <label class="toggle"><input type="checkbox"> Disable the field</label>
          <code class="field" aria-hidden="true"></code>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
    <section class="colophon" aria-labelledby="how">
      <div class="colophon-head"><h2 id="how">How it is built</h2><a class="down" href="#how-text" aria-label="Read how it is built"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 4v15M6 13l6 6 6-6"/></svg></a></div>
      <div id="how-text">
      <p>The reels are plain CSS: each digit is a column 0–9 plus a second 0, moved by <code>mod()</code> and <code>round()</code> of a registered number that eases toward the count, so 99 → 100 turns all three wheels together and leading zeros fold away. <a href="https://reactivecss.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Reactive CSS</a> adds the interaction, with no application JavaScript:</p>
      <ul><li>The count is a <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/state/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>state()</code></a>; − and + are <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/on-rule/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(click)</code></a> rules that <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/set/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>set()</code></a> it within its range. <code>bind:</code> keeps a hidden number field in step with it, so it submits with the form.</li><li>Hold a button to repeat: while it is held, a short animation runs, and each <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/on-rule/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(animationend)</code></a> steps the count and flips a state that swaps the animation, so the next one starts.</li><li>v2: drag the number like a dial. <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/drag/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(drag(start))</code></a> remembers the count, and <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/drag/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:on(drag)</code></a> sets it from <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/event/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>event(delta-y)</code></a>, one step every 10px.</li><li>v3, for forms: <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/state/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>state(attr(data-max, 999))</code></a> reads the form's range and step as numbers, and <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/attr-property/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>--attr-disabled</code></a> disables the buttons when the field is disabled.</li><li>Each hint fades after its first real use, through a page-wide <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/state/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>state()</code></a> and <a href="https://reactivecss.com/docs/reference/when-selector/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><code>:when()</code></a>.</li></ul>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>
  <div class="labels">
    <span class="label-made">Built with <b>Reactive CSS</b></span>
    <a class="label-link" href="https://reactivecss.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener">reactivecss.com ↗</a>
  </div>
  <div class="labels left">
    <a class="label-x" href="https://x.com/troshkin_pavel" target="_blank" rel="noopener">𝕏 @troshkin_pavel</a>
  </div>
</body>
</html>



/*
Rolling Counter: a stepper whose digits roll like an odometer, in three versions (click and hold, a drag dial, forms), with CSS and Reactive CSS.

  https://reactivecss.com
  https://github.com/troshkinpavel/reactivecss
  https://x.com/troshkin_pavel

No application JavaScript. The only script is the Reactive CSS runtime, loaded in the HTML: https://unpkg.com/@reactivecss/core@0.9.1/dist/reactive-css.min.js
keep the citation
Everything the page does is in style.css.
*/

Set up dither-kit for all the graphs and chart — composable dithered charts + avatars/buttons/gradients for shadcn/ui — in this project with bun as the isntaller but local fiels in raw chadcn
tracked URL (counts as an install) or the GitHub shorthand:



npx shadcn@latest add https://tripwire.sh/r/area-chart.json



npx shadcn@latest add Boring-Software-Inc/dither-kit/area-chart
migrate for Tailwind to vanilla + a shadcn project (components.json) to existing non shadcn migration. Install with the Dither Kit CLI (pulls the shared `core` engine + deps like motion and d3 automatically; writes a lockfile so you can update later):

  npx @dither-kit/cli add area-chart

  npx @dither-kit/cli list



Files land in components/dither-kit/. The API is recharts-style / children-as-config — a data array plus a config object that maps each series to a label and colour:



  import { AreaChart, Area, XAxis, YAxis, Legend, Tooltip } from "@/components/dither-kit/area-chart"



  const data = [{ month: "Jan", desktop: 186 }, { month: "Feb", desktop: 240 }]

  const config = { desktop: { label: "Desktop", color: "blue" } }



  <AreaChart data={data} config={config} bloom="aura">

    <XAxis dataKey="month" />

    <YAxis />

    <Legend isClickable />

    <Tooltip labelKey="month" />

    <Area dataKey="desktop" variant="gradient" />

  </AreaChart>



Other charts: bar-chart, pie-chart, radar-chart (or `npx @dither-kit/cli add dither-kit` for everything).

- variant: gradient | dotted | hatched | solid

- color: green blue purple pink orange red grey

- bloom: off | low | high | aura



Three standalone extras (no chart engine, tiny installs — same CLI):

- button — dithered native buttons: <DitherButton color="blue" variant="gradient">label</DitherButton>

- gradient — dithered background washes: <DitherGradient from="purple" direction="up" /> inside a relative container
